Sections: 01=`c8f11638‑…`, 02=`b279d5ab‑…`, 03=`eea83b7c‑…`, 04=`5405123f‑…`; head‑rows 01=`15d9016d`,02=`e2879de5`,03=`633bbc00`,04=`a287ae3c`.

**⚠️ LESSONS (reusable):**
- **`element_snapshot` does NOT render a freshly‑swapped image asset** (Designer‑canvas cache): the glyph area came back BLANK right after `set_image_asset` even though the CDN served the file (`curl` 200). **Verify an asset swap via the PUBLISHED page instead:** curl `/about`, grep the `<img>` srcs, extract the compiled CSS (Webflow writes MULTI‑LINE rules → use a DOTALL `\.sel\s*\{.*?\}` regex, not line‑based grep), and render the published HTML offline with the local glyph SVGs inlined as `data:` URIs (Webflow‑CDN images don't load through the proxy — §28).
- **Pick font sizes by MEASURING** the real face in the headless harness (render label → `getBoundingClientRect`), never by guessing.
- **Asset upload guard:** before the S3 POST, base64‑decode each presigned `policy` → JSON → assert its embedded `key` == the `key` field (catches transcription corruption, §30). All 4 → HTTP 201. Field mapping per `webflow://guides/asset-upload`: camelCase → `X-Amz-*`, `policy`→`Policy`.
- **Responsive alignment — FINALIZED as a first‑line anchor (2026‑07‑07; SUPERSEDES the earlier `flex-end`).** User rule: the glyph must NEVER stack below the text, and its bottom row must stay on the title's FIRST line **no matter how many lines the title wraps to**. Two changes: (1) `.if-stage-head-row` → `flex-wrap:nowrap` + `.if-stage-head-txt` `min-width:0` so the glyph stays LEFT and the TEXT wraps beside it (the graphic never drops below). (2) `.if-stage-head-row` → `align-items:flex-start` and each glyph gets a computed `margin-top` that pulls its BOTTOM ROW onto the first title line: `margin-top: calc(D − <glyph‑bottom‑row‑offset>)` where **D = first‑title‑line bottom from the row top = `clamp(11px,1vw,13px)` (eyebrow line) + `clamp(4px,0.6vw,8px)` (head‑txt gap) + `clamp(30px,3.6vw,50px)` (title line)**. Per glyph: base `if-stage-glyphimg` (01/02, bottom‑row = glyph bottom) subtracts `clamp(54px,5.9vw,86px)`; `if-glyph-tall` (04) subtracts `clamp(85px,9.2vw,134px)`; `if-glyph-line` (03, bottom‑row = box row, belt below) subtracts `clamp(24px,2.6vw,38px)` (≈0.667×glyph height) and sets `margin-bottom:0` (belt now hangs naturally). Because `flex-start` fixes the glyph's TOP to the row top independent of the text‑block height, wrapped title lines grow DOWNWARD while the glyph's bottom row stays put on line 1. Verified on the published CSS at 1440/700 (single line) and 430 (all four titles wrap to 2 lines) — box row = FIRST line in all 4, no stacking, glyph left. ⭐ Do NOT revert to `flex-end` (that anchored to the LAST wrapped line).

### 31E. Stage number WATERMARK behind each bar (2026‑07‑07) — native Designer, PUBLISHED
User wanted the original big translucent stage number brought back WITHOUT disturbing the (hard‑won) glyph/title alignment. Built as an OUT‑OF‑FLOW element so it can't push anything.
- New global class **`if-stage-wm`** on a text element per bar (text `01`‑`04`): `position:absolute; right:0; bottom:0; z-index:0; font-size:clamp(120px,18vw,280px); font-weight:800; line-height:0.8; letter-spacing:-0.04em; color:rgba(255,255,255,0.10); white-space:nowrap; pointer-events:none; user-select:none`. Combo **`if-wm-dark`** = `color:rgba(0,0,0,0.10)` for the gold stage‑04 bar. Watermark element ids: 01 `acb83037‑…`, 02 `b0253a83‑…`, 03 `b9cc9fe8‑…`, 04 `3f3ddb9b‑…`.
- **RIGHT‑justified** (`right:0` = same gutter on the right as the content has on the left, since it lives inside `if-stage-head-row` within the 24px‑padded `if-stage-wrap`), **baseline‑anchored** (`bottom:0` = row bottom = the title first line, the same anchor the glyph uses), **behind** everything (`z-index:0`; the glyph + `if-stage-head-txt` were given `position:relative; z-index:1`), and independent (absolute → cannot shift the aligned pieces). `if-stage-head-sec` got `overflow:hidden` so the oversized number clips at the bar edges instead of bleeding into adjacent sections.
- ⚠️ **`data_element_builder` type `TextBlock` came out as a plain DivBlock carrying Webflow's default placeholder String ("This is some text inside of a div block."), and the builder's inline `set_text` did NOT apply** → it first rendered the placeholder. `set_text` on the DivBlock errors ("element doesn't support text"); the FIX is `set_text` on its **String child** (id = the parent element id with its last hex digit +1). Always re‑query the String child's `textContent` after building a TextBlock.
- Dials: opacity on `if-stage-wm` (10%), size `clamp(120px,18vw,280px)`, right gutter (`right:0`).

### 31F. Stage headers — cross‑bar alignment via a fixed glyph COLUMN (2026‑07‑07) — native Designer, PUBLISHED
User: the four stacked bars read as ragged because the glyphs have different widths (2×2 ≈ 86px vs the wider staircase/assembly ≈ 134px), so the eyebrow+title started at a different x on each bar. Fix = make `.if-stage-head-row` a **CSS grid** with a constant first column so text starts at the SAME x on every bar, at every breakpoint. **This SUPERSEDES the earlier flex `nowrap` layout** (the §31 responsive bullet's "(1) flex-wrap:nowrap" clause; the first‑line `margin-top` anchor from "(2)" is unchanged and still works in grid).
- `.if-stage-head-row` → `display:grid; grid-template-columns: clamp(85px,9.2vw,134px) 1fr; align-items:start;` (first column = the widest glyph's width; `grid-column-gap` kept).
- `.if-stage-glyphimg` → `justify-self:start` — the glyph keeps its NATURAL width (2×2 stays `clamp(54px,5.9vw,86px)`; the wide glyphs fill the column) and left‑aligns within the fixed column, so the narrow glyphs sit left with extra slack before the text.
- The margin‑top first‑line anchor still applies (grid `align-items:start` = top; the per‑glyph `margin-top` calc offsets the glyph so its bottom row lands on the first title line). The watermark stays an absolute child of the row (unaffected — grid doesn't place absolute items).
- **Verified on the published CSS:** all 4 bars' eyebrow AND title left edges identical at desktop (x=285) and mobile (x=125); glyph left edges also align (page margin); each glyph's bottom row still locks to its title's first line; nothing stacks. ⭐ Cross‑bar text alignment holds at all widths.

New classes this session: `if-stage-wm`, `if-wm-dark`, `if-glyph-line`, `if-glyph-tall`, `if-strong` (no new classes for §31F — grid/`justify-self` are properties on existing classes). Everything except the §31‑A bold rule is native Designer (published to staging) → `stable` unaffected; the bold rule awaits promotion to `main`.

### 31G. Stage‑header glyph — box‑row → title BASELINE (not line‑box bottom) + black watermark 20% (2026‑07‑08) — native Designer, PUBLISHED
User (furious, several rounds): the glyph box‑row still wasn't lining up with the title; "VERIFY IT'S RIGHT before you present." Root cause found by MEASURING: the §31 `margin-top` formula anchored the box‑row to **`D` = the bottom of the title's line‑box** (`clamp(11px,1vw,13px)+clamp(4px,.6vw,8px)+clamp(30px,3.6vw,50px)`), which is **13–16px BELOW the text baseline** — so every glyph's box‑row floated that far above where the letters actually sit. Uniform across all 4 bars (so relatively consistent), just offset.
- **FIX:** shift the title term from `1.0×fs` → **`0.85×fs + 20.5px`**, i.e. add a correction that scales with font size. Rewritten as pure clamps (no `*` — safer for Webflow's calc parser; scaling a clamp by a positive constant scales all three args): the title term `clamp(30px,3.6vw,50px)` became **`clamp(25.5px,3.06vw,42.5px) + 20.5px`**. So the three glyph `margin-top`s are now:
  `calc(clamp(11px,1vw,13px) + clamp(4px,.6vw,8px) + clamp(25.5px,3.06vw,42.5px) + 20.5px − <glyph‑term>)`
  where `<glyph‑term>` = `clamp(54px,5.9vw,86px)` (base 01/02), `clamp(85px,9.2vw,134px)` (`if-glyph-tall` 04), `clamp(24px,2.6vw,38px)` (`if-glyph-line` 03; = 0.667×its height, box‑row above the belt). This yields the SAME correction the measurement demanded: **+16px at fs=30 (≤768px), +13px at fs=50 (1440px)**, transitioning between.
- **VERIFY METHOD (the reliable one — use this for any glyph/text vertical alignment):** offline harness renders the **actual live published compiled CSS** (re‑curl `/about` after publish → new hash → fetch it; NO override) + real Interstate woff2 + cropped glyph data‑URIs. Measure the title **baseline exactly** via a zero‑size inline probe: `span{display:inline-block;width:0;height:0;vertical-align:baseline}` inserted as the title's first child → its `getBoundingClientRect().top` = first‑line baseline (font‑metric‑exact, no guessing). box‑row‑bottom = `glyphRect.top + frac×glyphRect.height` (`frac`: 01/02/04 = 1.0, 03 = 0.667 — exact from the cropped SVG viewBoxes). delta = box‑row‑bottom − baseline. Scripts: `genharness.py` (builds `stagebars.html`), `measurebars.js` (measures + draws cyan box‑row / green baseline guide lines + per‑bar screenshots), `cleanshot.js` (clean stacked proof `PROOF_desk.png`/`PROOF_mob.png`) in scratchpad.
- **VERIFIED (live published CSS):** delta = **0px at 430 / 768 / 1440, 0.8px at 992**, uniform across all 4 bars; LOOKED at all four at desktop AND mobile (`PROOF_*.png`) — box‑row bottom sits exactly on each title's first‑line baseline, stage‑03 belt hangs below, glyph stays LEFT with text wrapping beside it (never stacks). ⭐ Target is the **baseline**, NOT the line‑box bottom — don't regress to the `1.0×fs` form.
- **⚠️ LESSON (process):** the user's angry screenshot was the **pre‑publish / browser‑cached** state — after publishing, re‑curl the fresh compiled CSS (hash changes) and verify THAT, and tell the user to hard‑refresh. Don't argue from the stale image.
- **Watermark opacities — PER‑BAR combos on `if-stage-wm` (base = white 10% `#ffffff1a`).** CONFIRMED first (pixel‑measured, clean isolation) that all four were genuinely **10%** (`if-stage-wm` = `#ffffff1a`; stage‑04 `if-wm-dark` = black `#0000001a`; measured 10.2% on gold). 10% white on pure black = **RGB(26,26,26)** — highest RAW luminance delta (26 vs 0, exactly as the user reasoned) but sRGB gamma crushes near‑black so it READS faintest. So the user dialed individual bars via combo classes (element ids §31E):
  - **Stage 01 (red)** → **15%**: combo **`if-wm-15`** (`rgba(255,255,255,0.15)` → compiled `#ffffff26`), applied `set_style ["if-stage-wm","if-wm-15"]` to `acb83037‑af0e‑624e‑dc36‑b2e5df63d333`.
  - **Stage 02 (maroon)** → stays base **10%** (plain `if-stage-wm`).
  - **Stage 03 (black)** → **18%**: combo **`if-wm-bright`** (`rgba(255,255,255,0.18)` → compiled `#ffffff2e`) on `b9cc9fe8‑25d8‑f7c7‑6d0d‑5312d211b313`. (Was briefly 20%, then user → 18%.)
  - **Stage 04 (gold)** → **10% black**: combo `if-wm-dark`.
  - **Dial per bar = the white/black alpha on that bar's combo** (`if-wm-15`, `if-wm-bright`, `if-wm-dark`) or the base `if-stage-wm` for stage 02. Verified live in the compiled CSS (hex‑alpha exact) + rendered (`PROOF_desk.png`).
- No shared‑file change → `stable` unaffected. All native Designer, published to staging. New classes: `if-wm-bright` (18%), `if-wm-15` (15%).

### 31H. 🔒 Stage‑header VERTICAL LAYOUT — LOCKED (2026‑07‑08) — native Designer, PUBLISHED & USER‑APPROVED. DO NOT CHANGE.
User approved this and said **"LOCK IN THAT VERTICAL SPACING. I DO NOT want that changed again."** Two native Designer edits (published, hash `e348b3a97`). **Treat as frozen** — do not touch `if-stage-wm` `top`/`bottom` or `if-stage-head-sec` `padding-top`/`padding-bottom` without an explicit user request.

**The invariant (what "right" means here):** on every bar, at every breakpoint, three things share ONE horizontal line — (a) the glyph's **bottom row of squares**, (b) the **first line** of the big title, (c) the **watermark's baseline**. AND each cluster is **vertically centered** in its bar (equal top/bottom margins, uniform across all 4 bars).

1. **Watermark baseline → first‑line baseline (wrapping‑proof).** `if-stage-wm` was `bottom:0` (row bottom) → drifted to the LAST line when titles wrapped on mobile. FIX: anchor from the **row top** (fixed regardless of wrapping) using the glyph's OWN baseline formula minus the watermark's ascent:
   `.if-stage-wm{ top: calc(clamp(11px,1vw,13px) + clamp(4px,.6vw,8px) + clamp(25.5px,3.06vw,42.5px) + 20.5px − clamp(91.2px,13.68vw,212.8px)); bottom:auto; }`
   - The first 4 terms = **B**, the first‑line‑baseline offset from row‑top — *identical* to the glyph's §31G `D` (verified 84px@1440 / 61px@430). The last term = the watermark's **ascent A = 0.76 × font** (font = `clamp(120px,18vw,280px)` → `clamp(91.2,13.68vw,212.8)`; A/font measured 0.758–0.760 constant). So watermark baseline = row‑top + B = first‑line baseline = glyph box‑row. **Because it's measured from the row top, wrapping can't move it.**
2. **Cluster centered via section padding (moves the WHOLE cluster together — never any piece alone).** The watermark (anchored high, at the first line) makes the cluster top‑heavy; a downward shift `S` centers it. `S` tracks the watermark size (its overhang above the first line): `S = 0.266 × font − 18.4px = clamp(31.92px,4.788vw,74.48px) − 18.4px` (fits +13.5px@mobile … +50.5px@desktop). Redistributed symmetrically so **bar height is unchanged**:
   `.if-stage-head-sec{ padding-top: calc(clamp(60px,7.5vw,110px) + clamp(31.92px,4.788vw,74.48px) − 18.4px); padding-bottom: calc(clamp(60px,7.5vw,110px) − clamp(31.92px,4.788vw,74.48px) + 18.4px); }`
   - ⭐ **Changing top/bottom margins = ONLY touch this section padding.** It moves watermark+glyph+text as one rigid block, so it CANNOT break the internal alignment (that's the "won't fuck it up again" guarantee the user demanded). Never re‑position the watermark/glyph/text individually to change margins.
- **VERIFIED (live published CSS, `checkwm.js`/`mmshot.js` in scratchpad):** watermark baseline − first‑line baseline = **±0.2px** at 1440/1200/992/768/600/480/390 (incl. wrapped titles — stage 2/3 wrap to 2–3 lines on mobile, watermark stays on line 1); glyph box‑row − first‑line = ~0; margins centered T≈B and TOP uniform across bars at every breakpoint (e.g. mobile ~T49/B49, desktop ~T59/B58). LOOKED at desktop+mobile proofs — watermark on the first line, clusters centered. **User approved.**
- **LESSONS (hard‑won this session):** (a) the watermark must anchor to the **row TOP**, not row bottom, or it follows the last wrapped line. (b) `getClientRects().length` is **1 for a block element even when its text wraps** — don't use it to count title lines; confirm wrapping via `offsetHeight/line-height`. (c) `mmshot` clip height must be `barH−3` or the screenshot catches the **next** bar's edge → false `BOTTOM=0`. (d) baseline is measured font‑exactly via a zero‑size `vertical-align:baseline` inline probe. (e) The user could see my Read‑tool renders — showing a WRONG proposed render (I'd centered the watermark, breaking its baseline alignment) caused real anger; **only surface renders that are verified correct.**
- No shared‑file change → `stable` unaffected. **NEXT on stage headers = HORIZONTAL spacing of items (user's next task).**

### 31I. Stage‑header HORIZONTAL spacing — glyphs right‑aligned + text = one skipped mini‑square (2026‑07‑08) — native Designer, PUBLISHED
User's spec (03 & 04 have the WIDEST glyphs → the reference; derive 01/02 from them). Two native Designer edits (published, hash `085c01a15`). Watermarks explicitly left untouched.
1. **ALL glyphs share ONE right edge (right‑aligned, not left).** The grid col‑1 width is already the widest‑glyph width (`clamp(85px,9.2vw,134px)` = the 03/04 `if-glyph-line`/`if-glyph-tall` width). Changed **`.if-stage-glyphimg` `justify-self: start → end`** so the narrow 2×2 glyphs (01/02, width `clamp(54,5.9vw,86)`) right‑align to the col‑1 right edge — same right edge as 03/04, which fill the column. So 01/02 are now indented on the LEFT (glyphL 47.5 desktop / 31 mobile) but their right edge = 132.5/85 like 03/04. (Combos inherit `justify-self` from the base, so all four align.)
2. **Text starts one skipped mini‑square right of the shared glyph edge.** Project the glyph's mini‑square grid rightward; skip square‑1, align text with square‑2's left edge → offset = **square + 2·gap**. From the 03/04 grid (viewBox 106u: square `S=30u`, gap `G=8u`, so `S+2G=46u`): offset = `46/106 × col1width` = **`clamp(36.9px, 3.99vw, 58.15px)`**. Applied as **`.if-stage-head-row` `grid-column-gap`** (col‑1 right edge = the shared glyph right edge, so the column‑gap IS the glyph‑to‑text skip). Because the grid col‑1 width and gap are the same for every bar, **all four bars' text‑left is identical** (189.9px desktop / 121.9px mobile) — the point of the exercise ("all text aligns precisely across bars"). Both the eyebrow row (dash + `STAGE 0X`) and the title start there.
3. **Watermarks untouched** — they're `right:0` (row right edge); neither `justify-self` nor `column-gap` moves them. Verified `wmR`=0 (unchanged).
- **VERIFIED (live published CSS, `measH.js` in scratchpad):** glyphR uniform 132.5/85 across all 4; dashL=titleL uniform 189.9/121.9; skip = 57.5/36.9 (=S+2G); wmR=0; LOOKED at desktop+mobile (`PROOF_*.png`) — 01/02 indented with right edges matching 03/04, text one‑square‑skip and aligned across bars, watermark in place. The §31H LOCKED vertical (glyph `margin-top`, `align-items:start`, `grid-template-columns`) is intact — only `justify-self` + `grid-column-gap` (horizontal) changed.
- No shared‑file change → `stable` unaffected. **Dials:** glyph right‑alignment = `justify-self` on `if-stage-glyphimg`; glyph‑to‑text skip = `grid-column-gap` on `if-stage-head-row` (currently S+2G = `clamp(36.9px,3.99vw,58.15px)`).

## 32. ABOUT program‑card + section polish batch (2026‑07‑08) — native Designer + 1 shared‑CSS line, PUBLISHED (shared CSS promoted to `main`)
Rapid user‑iterated card/section refinements on the About page. All verified (offline harness `behav.html`/`cardfix.html` + live compiled CSS).
- **A. Program‑card red top — RECTILINEAR, no miter (native, combo `if-prog-card-stage`).** The red top was `border-top:3px solid #e21833` which **mitered** with the base `if-prog-card` `border:1px #e6e6e6` (3px‑vs‑1px corner = diagonal). A transparent‑border attempt then mitered the GRAY. FINAL: `border-top-width:0` + draw the red as a **background gradient** — `background-image:linear-gradient(#e21833,#e21833); background-repeat:no-repeat; background-size:100% 3px; background-position:0% 0%`. Background rectangles don't miter → **square red ends, flush at top; gray sides square (no top border to miter with) → normal rectilinear box.** ⭐ RULE: a border adjacent to a different‑width border ALWAYS miters (diagonal corner); to get a square‑ended top accent, draw it as a background/element, not a border.
- **B. Gold pull‑quote band spacing (native, combo `if-note-gold`).** It sat directly on the card grid; added `margin-top:clamp(32px,4vw,56px)`.
- **C. Card "VISIT PROGRAM ››" bottom‑aligned across each row (1 shared‑CSS line + native).** Cards are grid items (equal height per row). Native `if-prog-visit{margin-top:auto}` pushes the link to the bottom — BUT only works if its flex parent fills the card. The JS‑inserted `.if-prog-bodyinner` (the hover‑grow wrapper) was content‑height, so shared CSS `.if-prog-bodyinner` gained **`flex-grow:1`** (idea‑factory.css) so it fills `.if-prog-body` (already `flex-grow:1`). Now visit sits at the card bottom → aligns across the row. Verified `behav.html`: two different‑height cards → both visit tops at y=339. Harmless on Students/Faculty cards (no `if-prog-visit`; content stays top‑aligned).
- **D. Stage teaser hanging indent (native, `if-stage-teaser-txt`).** The `↓` arrow was an inline char, so wrapped lines fell UNDER the arrow. FIX: `if-stage-teaser-txt{display:flex;align-items:baseline}` (+ `if-teaser-arrow{flex-shrink:0}`) → arrow + text become flex items; the text wraps within its own item, so wrapped lines align with the FIRST word after the arrow (hanging indent). ⭐ Reusable pattern for "icon + text, wrapped text aligns under text not icon": flex the container.
- **E. ⭐ FONT RULE — small white‑on‑dark text = Interstate, NOT Georgia (user, 2026‑07‑08).** Georgia's thin serifs read badly small + white‑on‑black. **Reserve small Georgia for BLACK‑on‑WHITE (dark‑on‑light) body copy only.** Applied: `cta-fine` (CTA band fine print, white‑on‑black) → `font-family:Interstate,"Helvetica Neue",Arial,sans-serif` (kept italic + the `rgba(255,255,255,0.6)` color). The other non‑`if` serif classes stay Georgia because they're dark‑on‑light (`premise-lead`/`syn-loop-txt`/`move-desc` — on white/light panels). **Going forward: any small text on a dark bg uses Interstate (or an `if-` sans class); Georgia only on light bg.**
- **Deploy:** A/B/D/E are native Designer → published to staging. C's shared‑CSS line (`if-prog-bodyinner flex-grow:1`) + the pending §31A bold‑serif rule were **promoted: `idea-factory.css` pushed to `main`** (commit `b473eba`, targeted file promotion per OPERATING PROTOCOL §2) → GitHub Pages serving; also on dev. `stable` NOT advanced (advance after user confirms live).

**➡️ (§32 PENDING items are now DONE — see §33.)**

## 33. ABOUT animations batch (2026‑07‑08) — 3 new shared‑JS modules + 1 shared‑CSS rule + native Designer, PUBLISHED + promoted to `main`
Delivered the pending scroll/read animations plus small fixes. **Shared JS/CSS promoted to `main`** (GitHub Pages, commit `78afc43`; also dev `3c5b19d`) AND **Webflow published together** (native marker/clip/overflow changes) per the §18 coordination rule. Verified offline (`verify_anim.js`/`shot_reader.js`, 0 JS errors) AND live (curled: live h2 carries the markers; GitHub Pages serves all 3 new modules + the CSS). `stable` NOT advanced (advance after the user confirms live).

- **A. "We do that work in four stages." = footer‑CTA read‑reveal (NEW module `read-line`).** User: "same animation as 'Have an idea? Let's build it' in the footer." Any **`.if-readline`** heading gets the word‑by‑word read: on first **mouseenter of its section**, words dim (0.62) then brighten one‑by‑one (D=180/gap=40/lastExtra=640/tail=150, then a 2.4s `rl-settling` rise to full — same timing family as `initCta`), and a **wrapped accent child** (here `<span class="if-mani-red">four stages.</span>`) **reddens as the read reaches it and HOLDS** (`if-lit-red`). Words are split at RUNTIME (source stays natively editable — same trick as `initCta`). **Native changes:** created inert marker **`if-readline`** (id `d10ec663…`), `set_style` the h2 (`5f54e2d1…`) → `["if-manifesto","if-readline"]`, and the accent span (`afd755c1…`) `if-callout-red`→**`if-mani-red`** (the existing inert‑red marker: `.if-mani-red{color:inherit;transition:color .45s}` + `.if-mani-red.if-lit-red{color:#e21833}` — so it starts BLACK, turns red on read). **Shared CSS:** `.if-rl-word{transition:opacity .28s cubic-bezier(.22,1,.36,1)}` + `.rl-settling .if-rl-word{transition-duration:2.4s}`. ⚠️ Reader targets ONLY `.if-readline`; the page's `initManifesto` still keys off the FIRST `.if-manifesto` (the Premise callout) so there's no collision. ⭐ Reusable: put `if-readline` on a heading + wrap the accent phrase in `if-mani-red` → done.
- **B. Gold bars (5) — Hero cut‑angle + scroll width (NEW module `about-goldbars`).** User: right end gets a **cut‑off angle like the Hero bar** + the Hero bar's **scroll effect**. **Native:** added `clip-path:polygon(0 0, calc(100% - 1.75px) 0, 100% 100%, 0 100%)` (the exact Hero `if-hero-goldbar` value) to base classes **`if-stg-goldbar`** (all 4 stage‑photo bars) and **`if-syn-goldbar`** (synthesis) → subtle angled right end. **Shared JS:** `about-goldbars` selects `.if-stg-goldbar,.if-syn-goldbar` and animates each bar's **width 30%→90%** as it scrolls into view (`p=(vh-r.top)/(vh+r.height)`, MIN=30/MAX=90 — identical math to the Hero `if-hero-goldbar` module). Overrides the native 44% resting width with an inline width. **Dials:** MIN/MAX in the module; the 1.75px cut in the two clip‑paths.
- **C. Synthesis "One place. Every stage." scroll (NEW module `syn-moment`).** User: same scroll effects as the HOME "Every stage" section. Independent module (Home's `if-sm-box` `initStage` left untouched per §5). On `.if-syn-hero`: the text block **`.if-syn-inner`** drifts down (amp 200, over 48, tt .68) + scales to 1.05 + fades in (opacity `p²`); the bg photo **`.if-syn-bg`** zooms **1.0→1.16** on scroll. `if-syn-hero` already has `overflow:hidden` (clips the zoom). Verified: inner `translateY(48px) scale(1.039)` opacity .44 mid‑scroll; bg `scale(1.15)`.
  - **⚠️ FOLLOW‑UP FIX (2026‑07‑08, commit `47377e8`): the scale MUST be anchored `transform-origin:left center`.** The first cut used the default center origin, so `scale(1.05)` grew the block outward and pushed its LEFT edge ~31px past the page gutter (measured innerLeft 100→69, headline 124→94) — the text visibly broke the consistent page margin. `if-syn-inner` is `if-stage-wrap` (max‑width 1240 + margin auto + 24px pad), correctly at left=100 / content=124 at 1440 with NO transform; the center‑origin scale was the ONLY cause (verified by measuring with/without the transform — `measure_syn.js`/`measure_syn_js.js`). Fix: `txt.style.transformOrigin='left center'` in the module → left edge stays at 100/125 (matches the reference `if-stage-wrap`) while the scale‑in grows rightward. ⭐ Faithful port of Home (Home anchors `right center` for its right‑aligned `if-stage-text`; anchor the text's OWN alignment edge). ⭐ RULE: a scroll/settle `scale()` on margin‑constrained content must anchor `transform-origin` to the aligned edge, or it eats the page margin. ⚠️ `width:100%` on `if-syn-inner` is CORRECT (so the flex‑column item fills to its max‑width then centers via margin auto) — do NOT remove it (removing → shrinks to content + recenters at 429). JS‑only fix; no Designer change.
- **D. CTA fine print bolder + not italic (native `cta-fine`).** User (twice). `font-weight:400→700`, `font-style:italic→normal` (kept Interstate + `rgba(255,255,255,0.6)`). Consistent with the §32‑E "small white‑on‑dark = Interstate" rule. **Dial:** font‑weight on `cta-fine`.
- **E. CTA red/gold top bar too tall in the Designer canvas (native `if-cta-topbar`).** User: displays thick in the Webflow UI, fine published. Classic **empty‑div inflation** (§28/§31E) — the flex bar is `height:4px` but its empty `if-cta-bar-red`/`-gold` children get min‑sized by the Designer and overflowed the 4px parent. **FIX: `overflow:hidden` on `if-cta-topbar`** → clips the inflated children to 4px in BOTH surfaces; published unchanged (nothing overflowed there). ⭐ Alternative to giving empty accent children explicit heights: clip them on the fixed‑height parent.

**⚠️ Still placeholder on About:** ~~per‑stage photos reuse the Stage‑01 lab photo~~ (RESOLVED — real per‑stage + synthesis photos wired, see §35); program‑card links `href="#"`; the 6 leftover Home‑duplicate sections between the CTA and footer still await the user's OK to delete (§29).

## 34. ABOUT stage‑photo L‑bracket + stage‑header canvas‑inflation diagnosis (2026‑07‑08) — native Designer, PUBLISHED
Two items. All native Designer (no shared‑file change → `stable` unaffected). Verified offline (`bars_test.js`) + Designer `element_snapshot`.

- **A. Stage‑photo accent bars — equal thickness + horizontal wins the corner (native, `if-stg-photo` + `if-stg-goldbar`; applies to all 4 stage photos via base classes).** User: make the red VERTICAL bar the same thickness as the gold HORIZONTAL bar, and flip the corner so the HORIZONTAL bar reaches the photo's LEFT edge and covers the bottom‑left corner (was: vertical border owned the corner). Structure: red vertical = `if-stg-photo` `border-left` (full height, incl. corner); gold horizontal = `if-stg-goldbar` (normal‑flow child, `height:12px`, `margin-top:-12px` sits on the photo bottom, `z-index:5`). **Changes:** (1) `if-stg-photo` `border-left-width 6px→12px` (matches the 12px gold). (2) `if-stg-goldbar` `margin-left:0→-12px` so its left edge lands at the photo's outer‑left (x=0), over the border. (3) `if-stg-photo` `overflow:hidden→visible` — REQUIRED so the gold's leftmost 12px (over the border region) isn't clipped at the padding edge. ⭐ **Safe because `if-stg-img` is `object-fit:cover` at 100%×100% → the image is clipped to its own box intrinsically, independent of the frame's `overflow`, so `overflow:visible` causes NO image bleed** (verified offline: img right/bottom stay within the frame). Net: gold (z‑index 5) paints over the red border's bottom‑left corner → horizontal overlaps vertical; red vertical visually stops at the gold's top. Both 12px. **Kept the gold's `clip-path` angle (§33‑B) + the `about-goldbars` width‑scroll (JS sets width%; margin‑left is native).** ⭐ RULE: to let a normal‑flow accent element paint over a parent's `border`, the parent needs `overflow:visible` (border sits outside the padding box, and `overflow:hidden` clips children at the padding edge). Used `overflow:visible` (not an absolute bar) to AVOID the §31E empty‑absolute‑div Designer inflation.
- **B. Stage‑header "bottom margin too big in the Designer" — diagnosed as a Designer‑preview artifact, NOT a published bug (no change shipped).** User saw the maroon/black stage‑header bars with an oversized bottom margin in the Webflow canvas though published is correct. **Measured (real Chromium, `measure_bar.js`): the bar is 307px tall with `padding-top≈159 / padding-bottom≈57` — IDENTICAL whether or not `idea-factory.css` loads (canvas‑equiv fallback font vs published Interstate both = 307/57).** So the shared CSS/font is NOT the cause. Root cause: the giant faded stage number `if-stage-wm` is `position:absolute` (height ~207px, negative `top`, clipped by `overflow:hidden`); a real browser lifts it out of flow so it adds ZERO height → published bar = exactly `padding+content`, correct. The **Webflow Designer canvas reserves space for that huge absolute element** (same family as the §28/§31E absolute/empty‑div inflation), so the bar previews taller with extra bottom room. **Tried `contain:layout paint` on `if-stage-head-sec`** (verified published no‑op = 307/57) — `element_snapshot` showed no improvement, so **reverted it** (didn't leave it on the §31H‑LOCKED section). **No safe fix exists that doesn't touch the LOCKED watermark position / padding, and published is correct — so left as a canvas‑preview‑only artifact.** ⭐ LESSON: a big `position:absolute` descendant can inflate the Webflow Designer canvas (preview only); confirm via a real‑browser measurement before treating it as a published bug.

Rapid user‑iterated batch. Shared JS promoted to `main` (commit `3804f40`; dev `3c5b19d`‑lineage) + Webflow published together (§18). Verified offline (`verify_scrollred.js`, `measure_headbar.js`) + live curl. `stable` not advanced (advance after user confirms).

"We do that work in four stages." + "That's what the Idea Factory is for." → SCROLL‑red, read reveal RETIRED (user: the read animation was "too much").** New shared‑JS module **`scroll-red`**: a heading with class **`if-scroll-red`** stays all‑black; its wrapped accent (`.if-mani-red` child) fades to red once the heading top passes **1/3 into the viewport** (`rect.top <= vh*2/3`) and HOLDS. No word‑read, no line‑lift. **Native:** four‑stages h2 (`5f54e2d1`) `if-readline`→`if-scroll-red`; premise callout h2 (`ddd110b5`) added `if-scroll-red`; premise "for." span (`acf0a1f2`) `if-callout-red`→**`if-mani-red`** (so it starts black, reddens on scroll — was permanently red). Created marker `if-scroll-red` (id `1f66cea8`). **JS also narrowed `initManifesto` → `document.querySelector('.if-manifesto:not(.if-scroll-red)')`** so it no longer line‑lifts these two About headings (Home manifesto unaffected — it has no `if-scroll-red`). Color transition rides the existing `.if-mani-red{transition:color .45s}` + `.if-mani-red.if-lit-red{color:#e21833}`. Verified: both start `rgb(0,0,0)`, redden on scroll, 4‑stages has 0 `.if-rl-word`, 0 JS errors. ⚠️ **The §33 `read-line` module + `.if-rl-word`/`.rl-settling` CSS are now UNUSED** (nothing has `if-readline`) — harmless (guarded, no‑ops), candidate for later removal.
- **B. Synthesis "One place. Every stage." gold bar — motion slowed (user: moving too fast, bigger distance than other instances).** `about-goldbars` module now uses a per‑bar max: `.if-syn-goldbar` travels **30%→60%** (range 30) vs the stage‑photo `.if-stg-goldbar` **30%→90%** (range 60) — half the distance → reads slower. One‑liner: `var mx=_b.classList.contains('if-syn-goldbar')?60:MAX`.
- **C. Synthesis section 50% TALLER (user: too short, building too cut‑off).** `if-syn-hero` `min-height` `clamp(340px,44vw,560px)` → **`clamp(510px,66vw,840px)`** (×1.5). Shows more of the building (the bg photo is object‑fit cover). **⚠️ REVISED 2026‑07‑08 (§38): 50% was too tall — reverted to the 340/44/560 original then +20% → LIVE value = `clamp(408px,52.8vw,672px)` (×1.2). Dial: `min-height` on `if-syn-hero`.**
- **D. Stage‑header gold bar — now ONLY on "01" (red); removed from "02"/"03" (user: becoming overused).** SUPERSEDES §35‑B. `remove_element` the `if-bar-wrap` overlays in stage 02 (`78239532`) + 03 (`abbcfa6e`); Stage 01's (`87f23540`) kept. **Watermark nudge re‑scoped:** base `if-stage-wm` `right` back to **0** (reverts 02/03/04), and the 16px nudge moved onto **`if-wm-15`** (Stage 01's watermark combo only, id `0aaffbf7`) so only the bar‑bearing header is nudged. (`position:relative` stays on the base `if-stage-head-sec` — needed for Stage 01's overlay, harmless on the others. On About HTML now **2** `if-proof-goldline`: Stage 01 + the leftover proof‑sec §29.)

## 38. ABOUT "Walk the Factory" wayfinding nav bar — BUILT + LIVE (2026‑07‑08) — native Designer + 1 shared‑CSS block + 4 SVG assets, PUBLISHED + promoted to `main`
Completed §37‑A. A horizontal wayfinding bar directly beneath the About Hero, before the Premise section: a **label box** ("WALK THE FACTORY" red + "Jump to a stage ›" gray) + **4 anchor cards** (01 BLUEPRINT / 02 PROVING GROUND / 03 ASSEMBLY LINE / 04 SCALE‑UP), each = greyscale glyph + num + name, each a smooth‑scroll anchor to its stage header. All verified offline (`walk_harness.js`) AND on the live published render with the REAL compiled Webflow CSS (`render_live_walk.js`), 0 JS errors.

- **Section:** `if-walk-sec` (`3a096333‑5614‑3ec7‑999f‑58fdd5ccb322`, after `if-hero-sec`, before the premise `if-mani-sec`) › `if-stage-wrap` (the 1240+24px page‑margin wrapper, §28) › `if-walk-row` (flex) › `if-walk-label` + 4× `if-walk-item` (LinkBlock). Card = `if-walk-glyph` (Image) + `if-walk-txt` › `if-walk-num` + `if-walk-name`.
- **Anchors (class‑driven smooth scroll):** each `if-walk-item` is `<a href="#stage-0X" data-smooth-scroll="1">`. The stage‑header sections carry DOM ids **`stage-01`…`stage-04`** (`set_dom_id` on `c8f11638`/`b279d5ab`/`eea83b7c`/`5405123f`). The shared‑JS smooth‑scroll module matches `a[data-smooth-scroll]`, resolves the target via `getElementById`, and offsets by the sticky `.if-header` height + 24px gap — verified it handles these (not just `#audience`). No JS change needed.
- **Glyphs = 4 real Webflow SVG assets** (transparent, full‑colour, tile‑normalized `viewBox 0 0 160 160`): 01 `6a4dfe9e568c61fbcfb6bc74`, 02 `6a4dfe9ec3e02d1f359d3b26`, 03 `6a4dfe9e25a51c671eb4763b`, 04 `6a4dfe9ed81613956be7c14b` (sources committed at `project/assets/glyphs/stage-0X-*-nav.svg`; on the Webflow CDN per the §30 asset rule).
- **REST look (all native Designer):** `if-walk-sec` white + 1px top/bottom border; `if-walk-item` bg `#f2f2f2`, 1px left divider, padding 16/18, `flex:1 1 0`, `column-gap 12`; `if-walk-glyph` 40×40 + **`filter:grayscale(1)`** (native → greyscale at rest; ⚠️ Webflow compiles `grayscale(1)`→`grayscale()`, which computes IDENTICALLY to `grayscale(1)` — verified on the live render); `if-walk-num` `#9a9a9a`/13px/700; `if-walk-name` `#5f5f5f`/15px/700/uppercase; label `if-walk-label-h` red 15px/700/0.08em, `if-walk-label-sub` `#7f7f7f`/14px.
- **HOVER = native box + shared parent‑hover‑child reveal.** Native `:hover` on `if-walk-item` → **`background:#fff` + a red LEFT bar** = `box-shadow:inset 3px 0 0 0 #e21833` (3px red inside the left edge) + `border-left-color:#e21833` (recolours the existing 1px grey divider red → a solid ~4px red left bar, no grey). ⚠️ **Was a TOP bar (`inset 0 3px`) — moved to the LEFT 2026‑07‑08 (user):** the top bar collided with the header/nav lines directly above the pinned bar. Both are inset shadows / border‑colour changes → **zero layout shift** (verified: glyph/num/name move `0px` on hover, `verify_leftbar.js`); text/glyphs never pushed. The parent‑hover‑child part CAN'T be native → **shared `idea-factory.css`** (after the `.rl-settling` block): `.if-walk-item:hover .if-walk-glyph{filter:none}` (glyph → full colour), `…:hover .if-walk-num{color:#e21833}` (num → red), `…:hover .if-walk-name{color:#111111}` (name → dark). Promoted to `main` (GitHub Pages, commit `e961bfe`; dev `c9e901a`) — confirmed served (40206 B). Same split as every card hover on the site (§3/§15).
- **RESPONSIVE ladder (native breakpoints; verified 1280/991/768/600/479/390/360, no overflow):** **main** = label + 4 cards in one row (5 columns). **medium (≤991)** = `if-walk-row` wraps → label on its OWN row (full‑width, +bottom border) then 4 cards across (avoids a cramped 5‑col near 768). **small (≤767)** = `if-walk-row` `gap:8px`, label full‑width no border + padding 0, `if-walk-item` `flex-basis:40%` + no left border → **2×2 grey cards**. **tiny (≤479)** = `if-walk-item` `flex-basis:100%` → **1‑column stack**. ⚠️ **Why tiny goes 1‑column:** at ~390px the single‑word names (BLUEPRINT, SCALE‑UP) hit their min‑content and broke the 2×2 into an ugly 1‑2‑1; a 1‑col stack is bulletproof + readable.
- **⚠️ Refinement (2026‑07‑08, user): removed `if-walk-sec` `border-top` (was 1px `#e6e6e6`).** On hover the red top accent is an inset shadow INSIDE the card, so the section's 1px grey top border showed ABOVE the red (grey line between the red and the black hero). Dropping the section border-top makes the red reach the black hero directly on hover — for ALL cards (global section change + `.if-walk-item:hover` is universal). Rest state now has no grey divider under the hero either (clean black→grey/white edge; the user explicitly didn't want that line). `border-bottom` (nav→premise divider) kept. Verified pixel‑level on the live render: `if-walk-sec` border-top `0px none`, cards at section‑top offset 0, red inset touches the hero on card 1 AND card 3. *(Note: once the hover accent moved to the LEFT (above), this top‑border removal is moot for the accent, but kept — it also gives a clean, dividerless black→bar edge at rest and when the bar is pinned under the header, §39.)*
- **New classes (all `if-`, native Designer):** `if-walk-sec, if-walk-row, if-walk-label, if-walk-label-h, if-walk-label-sub, if-walk-item, if-walk-glyph, if-walk-txt, if-walk-num, if-walk-name`. The 3 hover rules are the ONLY shared‑CSS addition.
- **Also this session:** synthesis `if-syn-hero` height revised 50%→+20% (see §36‑C). Both published to staging.
- **⚠️ Refinements deferred (per user "we can refine it later"):** rest label colours (I used `#9a9a9a`/`#5f5f5f`; the design ref `journey-bar-states.svg` uses `#bdbdbd` for inactive names) — leave unless asked. The design's per‑page "current stage in colour, rest grey" semantics were intentionally NOT used — the user asked for a HOVER‑driven nav (all grey at rest, colour on hover), which is what shipped.

**➡️ STILL PENDING (§37‑B, next up): first paragraph under each colored stage bar = BIGGER text (~1.5× `if-mani-p`), add e.g. `if-stagec-lead` to the first `if-mani-p` in each of the 4 stage bodies, fluid `clamp()`, responsive, native.** Also long‑standing: program‑card `href="#"` placeholders; the 6 leftover Home‑duplicate sections between the CTA and footer await the user's OK to delete (§29).

## 39. ABOUT "Walk the Factory" bar — STICKY through the stages (2026‑07‑08) — shared JS module, promoted to `main`
User: make the Walk the Factory nav bar persistent while scrolling down, until the NEXT section after the stages — "One place. Every stage." (the `if-syn-hero` synthesis band) — comes into view. NEW shared‑JS module **`walk-sticky`** (idea-factory.js; promoted to `main` commit `11072ac`, dev `51f9676`; GH Pages serving 55811 B). **Pure JS behavior on already‑published elements → NO Webflow publish needed** (keys off existing classes; sets inline styles + a data attr at runtime). `stable` not advanced (advance after user confirms).
- **Mechanism:** pins `.if-walk-sec` `position:fixed; top:<header height>` below the sticky `.if-header` once the bar's top reaches the header bottom; a **spacer div** (= bar height) is inserted to prevent the content jump. Stays pinned through Premise + the four stages. **Handoff/release:** when `.if-syn-hero`'s top rises to within the bar's height of the header bottom, the bar's `top` follows it up (`top = synTop − barH`) so the synthesis **pushes the bar up behind the header** — smooth, no pop. Fully reversible on scroll‑up (re‑pins, then unpins to normal flow when the spacer returns to the header line).
- **Header height is read dynamically** (`.if-header` getBoundingClientRect, = 179px on the live About page) each frame — robust to the responsive header.
- **⚠️ Desktop only (≥992)** where the bar is one slim row (~73px). On tablet/mobile the bar is 2 rows / 2×2 / 1‑col (too tall to pin), so it scrolls normally — the module `unpin()`s and skips below 992. (Lower the `active()` threshold if the user wants it on tablet.) **← SUPERSEDED by §40: the bar is now one slim row at ALL widths, and the gate is height‑based, so sticky pins at every width.**
- **⭐ Anchor‑gap fix:** the module also sets each `.if-walk-item`'s `data-scroll-gap` = `barH+24` on desktop (24 on mobile), so clicking a stage jump lands the stage header BELOW the pinned bar (not hidden behind it). The smooth‑scroll module reads `data-scroll-gap` at click time, so this stays accurate on resize.
- **Verified:** synthetic scroll harness (`sticky_test.js`) — pin at header bottom, stays pinned, handoff `top` rides 70→−40, reverses to `static`, desktop‑gated, gap 97/24, 0 errors; AND real published‑page render (`real_sticky_test.js`, header 179) — pinned `barTop=179`, handoff bar‑bottom flush on synthesis top (216=216), unpins at top, 0 errors; screenshot `real_pinned_vp.png` shows the bar pinned under the red nav header. **Dials:** `active()` width threshold; z‑index 40 (below header's 50); the `+24` gap padding.

## 40. ABOUT "Walk the Factory" bar — slimmed to nav height + one‑row/scroll + sticky at ALL widths (2026‑07‑08) — native Designer + shared CSS/JS, PUBLISHED + promoted to `main`
User: the bar is too tall — make it the SAME HEIGHT as the red site nav bar; keep the stage names on ONE line (scale text to fit, no wrapping); and the sticky pin must work after the bar "breaks" responsively. **SUPERSEDES §38's 2×2/1‑col responsive ladder AND §39's desktop‑only (≥992) sticky gate.**
- **Red nav bar height = 50px** (measured `.if-navmenu`). The walk bar is now **~51px at every width** (native Designer slimming): `if-walk-glyph` 40→**30px**, `if-walk-item` padding 16/18→**10/14**, `if-walk-num` 13→**11px**, `if-walk-name` 15→**13px + `white-space:nowrap`**, `if-walk-txt` row‑gap 3→**2**, `if-walk-label` padding→**6px** + `flex:0 0 auto`. **Label line spacing tightened to match the stage num/name (user, 2026‑07‑08): `if-walk-label-h` line‑height 1.15→**1**, `if-walk-label-sub` line‑height 1.2→**1.1** + margin‑top 3→**2px** → label two‑line gap now = stage num/name gap (2px), verified.** **"WALK THE FACTORY" red label matched to the "VISIT PROGRAM" red link (user, 2026‑07‑08): `if-walk-label-h` font‑size 15→**14px**, letter‑spacing 0.08em→**0.02em** (weight 700 / color #e21833 / uppercase already matched `if-prog-visit`) → computed styles now identical, fixing the spread‑out look.**
- **ONE slim row at ALL widths (no more reflow), buttons scale to content with CONSISTENT right margins.** `if-walk-item` = **`flex:1 0 auto`** (grow 1 / **shrink 0** / basis auto = content width; **no min-width**); `if-walk-row` **`overflow-x:auto`**. ⚠️ **Changed from `flex:1 1 0`+`min-width:196` (equal‑width boxes) → `flex:1 0 auto` on 2026‑07‑08 (user):** equal‑width boxes gave INCONSISTENT trailing space (short names like BLUEPRINT looked "empty", long ones like PROVING GROUND "cramped"). With `1 0 auto` each box's base = its content width and the leftover space is distributed **equally** (grow 1) → since content is left‑aligned (flex‑start), every box gets the **same trailing/right margin** (text‑end→box‑right), verified identical at 1440/1280/1024/768 (e.g. 109.5px each within the 1240 wrapper; 14px each when scrolling). On wide the boxes grow to fill; below ~910px the row **scrolls horizontally** (shrink 0 → never compress below content, names stay one line).
- **✅ SUPERSEDED (2026‑07‑08): content is now CENTERED in the one‑row layout too, not left‑aligned.** User: the stacked (mobile) buttons already center their content (§42's `justify-content:center` in the `@media(max-width:880px)` block) — do the same for the one‑row desktop layout so the "consistent trailing margin" bullet above no longer applies literally (the margin is still equal on both sides now, just because it's centered, not because of the flex‑grow trailing‑space mechanic). Native `update_style`: `if-walk-item` base gained **`justify-content:center`** (still `flex:1 0 auto` — unchanged; only the content's position within each grown box changed from flex‑start to centered). The `@media(max-width:880px)` rule's own `justify-content:center` is now redundant with the base but left in place (harmless, self‑documenting for that breakpoint). Verified: compiled CSS `.if-walk-item{…justify-content:center;…}`; Designer `element_snapshot` shows each button's glyph+text centered with balanced margins either side.
- **✅ FURTHER NUDGE (2026‑07‑08): centered content shifted ~10px LEFT, one‑row state ONLY.** User: nudge the (now‑centered) content slightly left in the largest/pre‑stacking state. Implemented via **asymmetric padding, not a transform** — native `if-walk-item` base `padding-right` `14px→34px` (left/top/bottom unchanged). Math: with `justify-content:center`, a flex item's content centers within its padding box, so content‑center offset from the button's own center = `(padding-left − padding-right)/2`; going from 14/14 (0 offset) to 14/34 gives `(14−34)/2 = −10px`. **Confined to the one‑row layout automatically, with ZERO shared‑CSS changes** — the pre‑existing `@media(max-width:880px)` block (§41) already fully overrides both `padding-left` AND `padding-right` back to a symmetric `12px`/`12px` for the stacked layout, so the nudge cascades away below 880px without touching that file. Considered a `transform:translateX(-10px)` alternative on the glyph+text elements instead, but rejected it — it would need setting on TWO separate sibling elements (glyph and txt aren't wrapped together) AND a shared‑CSS reset inside the stacked media query (which has no existing transform override) → more moving parts and an unnecessary shared‑file touch versus the one‑property native padding tweak. Verified with real pixel measurement (`verify_nudge.js`, live compiled CSS): content‑center offset = **‑9.5px at 1280px** (one‑row, all 4 buttons uniformly) vs **+0.5px at 700px** (stacked, unaffected); Designer `element_snapshot` confirms the visible leftward shift; 0 JS errors.
- **⭐ Full‑bleed RIGHT edge (user, 2026‑07‑08): buttons reach the browser right edge, no white gap.** The bar's wrapper is the shared `if-stage-wrap` (max‑width 1240, centered) so the row stopped at the 1240 content boundary; on the LEFT that gap is invisible (white label on white section bg) but on the RIGHT the grey buttons meet white → visible gap. Fix (native, walk‑only, does NOT touch shared `if-stage-wrap`): **`if-walk-row` `margin-right: calc(50% - 50vw)`** extends the row's right edge from the content‑right to the viewport‑right (50% = half the wrap content box, 50vw = half viewport; the diff = the right gap, at every width, continuously — ‑24px at ≤1240, more on wider). **`if-walk-sec` `overflow-x: clip`** clips the ~scrollbar‑width bleed so there's NO page horizontal scroll. LEFT edge stays at the content margin (label aligned with the page). Verified live 1440/1280: last button right = viewport inner width, labelLeft = content margin (124/44), 0 page H‑overflow; narrow (768) still scrolls, clipped. **Dial:** remove `margin-right` on `if-walk-row` (and `overflow-x` on `if-walk-sec`) to revert to the 1240‑capped bar. **Removed ALL the `medium`/`small`/`tiny` reflow overrides** (verified compiled CSS: 0 `@media` rules touch `if-walk`). Scrollbar hidden via shared CSS (`.if-walk-row{scrollbar-width:none}` + `::-webkit-scrollbar{display:none}`) so the pinned bar stays clean — non‑native pseudo → shared `idea-factory.css`.
- **Sticky now pins at ALL widths.** `walk-sticky` `active()` gate changed from `innerWidth>=992` to **height‑based: `barH>0 && barH < viewport*0.5`** — since the bar is always ~51px it always pins (and a degenerate tall bar still won't). Anchor `data-scroll-gap` = `barH+24` (~75) when active. Verified live‑render at **768px** (previously wouldn't pin): pins `barTop=129=headerBottom`, handoff bar‑bottom flush on synthesis top (155=155), names all 1 line, row scrolls; and 1280px: 51px, no scroll, pins at 179, handoff flush; **0 JS errors** (`final_walk_verify.js`, `final_pinned_768.png`).
- **Deploy:** native slim (glyph/padding/text/flex/min‑width/overflow) → Webflow published; shared CSS (scrollbar‑hide) + JS (height gate) → `main`/GitHub Pages (commit `832c03c`, dev `53d9859`; confirmed served). `stable` not advanced.
- **⚠️ Tradeoff / possible refinement:** on narrow **non‑touch** widths the horizontal scroll had no visible scrollbar — **RESOLVED by §41: below ~880px the bar now STACKS (2×2) instead of scrolling.**

## 41. ABOUT "Walk the Factory" bar — responsive STACK (2×2) below ~880px + bottom drop shadow (2026‑07‑08) — shared CSS media query + native shadow, PUBLISHED + promoted to `main`
Two user asks. **REPLACES the horizontal‑scroll‑on‑narrow behavior from §40** with a stack.
- **STACK below ~880px (user: "when the buttons can shrink no further, stack").** Measured with real Interstate: the one‑row layout's buttons hit their content‑min (can't shrink further) at **866px** → stack breakpoint set to **`@media (max-width:880px)`** (just above, so it stacks right as margins hit min, before any scroll). Custom breakpoint (Webflow only has 991/767/479) + a `::before` "|" pseudo → lives in **shared `idea-factory.css`** (not the Designer). Stacked layout: **label row on top, FULL‑WIDTH**, with `if-walk-label` → `flex-direction:row` so **"WALK THE FACTORY | Jump to a stage ›"** are inline (the "|" is `.if-walk-label-sub::before{content:"|"}`); then the four buttons become **two full‑browser‑width rows of two** (`if-walk-item` `flex-basis:50%` + `border-top` row dividers). **Full‑bleed BOTH edges** when stacked (base already bleeds right via `margin-right:calc(50% - 50vw)`; the media query adds `margin-left:calc(50% - 50vw)`) so the button rows span edge‑to‑edge; label text stays at the 24px content margin (`padding:8px 24px`). Stacked glyph 28px / name 12px so the **2×2 holds down to ~350px** (below that it gracefully degrades to 1‑col). Verified (real Interstate, `stack_proto.js`/`final_shot.js`): 1 row ≥880; clean 2×2 at 860/700/600/414/375; full‑bleed both edges; 0 page H‑overflow; sticky still pins the stacked bar (height‑gate, §40). The §40 one‑row + full‑bleed‑right + consistent‑margins all still apply ABOVE 880px.
- **Bottom drop shadow (user: same as the sticky header's).** Native Designer on `if-walk-sec`: **`box-shadow:0 4px 8px -2px rgba(0,0,0,0.06)`** (identical to the header bars, §23; compiled `#0000000f`) + `position:relative; z-index:2` so it paints OVER the content below (Premise). ⭐ **`overflow-x:clip` (from §40) does NOT clip the element's own bottom shadow** — verified it shows at rest, stacked, and when pinned. Separates the bar from the content below in every state.
- **Deploy:** shadow = native → Webflow published; stacking media query = shared CSS → `main`/GitHub Pages (commit `e1a3b9d`, dev `e1a3b9d`‑lineage; confirmed served). `stable` not advanced.
- **Dials:** stack breakpoint = the `880px` in the media query; 2×2‑vs‑1‑col floor = stacked glyph/name sizes; shadow = `box-shadow` on `if-walk-sec` (keep = header's value).

## 42. ABOUT "Walk the Factory" bar — CURRENT‑stage marker (scrollspy) (2026‑07‑08) — shared JS + CSS, promoted to `main`
User: give the buttons a "current" state = how it looks when the stage it links to is scrolled up. It should look like the HOVER state (white box, full‑colour glyph, red number, dark name) **but WITHOUT the red left bar** — the red left bar is the hover‑only cue that distinguishes hover from current.
- **NEW shared‑JS module `walk-spy`** (idea-factory.js): scrollspy that adds **`.is-current`** to the `.if-walk-item` whose linked `#stage-0X` head has scrolled up to within `headerH + barH + 12` of the top (i.e., sits just under the pinned bar); removes it from the rest. Keys off `.if-walk-item[href^="#stage-"]` + `getElementById` — class‑driven, portable. rAF‑throttled scroll/resize.
- **Shared CSS** (idea-factory.css, after the `:hover` rules): `.if-walk-item.is-current{background:#fff}` + `.is-current .if-walk-glyph{filter:none}` + `.is-current .if-walk-num{color:#e21833}` + `.is-current .if-walk-name{color:#111}`. **Deliberately NO box‑shadow / NO red border‑left** → keeps the plain grey divider. Hover rules layer on top, so **hovering a current button re‑adds the red left bar** (hover = current‑look + bar). ⭐ The red left bar is thus the ONLY visual difference: hover HAS it, current does NOT.
- **Pure JS + shared CSS on already‑published elements → NO Webflow publish.** Promoted CSS+JS to `main`/GitHub Pages (commit `b78154d`, dev lineage; confirmed served). `stable` not advanced.
- **Verified:** synthetic (`spy_test.js`) + REAL About page (`spy_live.js`): none current at top, button 01→02→03→04 becomes current as each stage scrolls up, 0 JS errors; current look = white / colour glyph / red num / dark name / grey divider / no shadow; current+hover = same + red left bar (`spy_current.png`).
- **Dial:** the current threshold (`headerH+barH+12`) in `walk-spy`.
- **⚠️ REVISED 2026‑07‑08 (user) — colour is HOVER‑only; the left bar is the CURRENT marker (grey→red).** New model (supersedes the "current = hover‑look minus bar" above): **only a HOVERED button is full colour** (white bg, colour glyph, red num, dark name) — current or not; **un‑hovered buttons are greyscale.** The **left bar is on every CURRENT button** and shares the greyscale/colour state: **grey `#555` when not hovered, red `#e21833` on hover.** So a non‑current button never has a bar; a current button always does. Implementation: removed the bar from the native `:hover` (now bg‑white only) and the colour rules from `.is-current`; `.is-current{box-shadow:inset 3px 0 0 0 #555555;border-left-color:#555555}` (grey bar) + `.is-current:hover{…#e21833}` (red bar); the `:hover` parent‑child colour rules (glyph/num/name) still supply the hover colour. Verified 4 states (`spy2.js`, 0 errors): rest=greyscale/no bar; hover‑not‑current=full colour/no bar; current‑not‑hover=greyscale/**grey bar**; current‑hover=full colour/**red bar** (`spy2_current.png`, `spy2_current_hover.png`). Promoted CSS `77c536b`. **Dial:** grey‑bar colour `#555555` on `.if-walk-item.is-current`.
- **⚠️ BUGFIX 2026‑07‑08 — marker landed on the PRECEDING button after a nav click (intermittent).** Root cause: `walk-spy` threshold was `headerH+barH+12`, but clicking a button smooth‑scrolls the target stage to `headerH + data-scroll-gap` where the gap = `barH+24` (§40 anchor‑gap) — so the just‑navigated stage's top landed ~12px BELOW the threshold line and the last stage ABOVE the line (the preceding one) read current; a few px of manual scroll "caught" it (hence intermittent). Fix: threshold now = **`headerH + <data-scroll-gap> + 12`** (reads the gap walk‑sticky sets on the items; fallback `barH+24`) so a landed stage is always ≥ the line → correct button. Verified at the true click‑landing position (`repro2.js`): stage 02→item1, 04→item3 at 1280 AND 700 (was preceding before). Promoted JS `5399330`.
- **Stacked buttons CENTER their content (user, 2026‑07‑08):** the full‑width stacked buttons looked odd left‑aligned (big right gap), so the `@media(max-width:880px)` `.if-walk-item` got **`justify-content:center`** (stacked only; the one‑row desktop keeps left‑aligned + consistent‑trailing per §40). Verified (`stack_centered.png`). Promoted CSS `5399330`.
- **⭐ Stacked PER‑COLUMN alignment (user, 2026‑07‑08): longest centered, shorter left‑aligns to it.** With just `justify-content:center`, each button centred its own content, so the two stacked buttons in a column (different name lengths) started at different x. NEW shared‑JS module **`walk-colalign`**: when stacked (2 rows), it groups the buttons by column (rounded `getBoundingClientRect().left`), finds each column's widest `.if-walk-txt`, and sets BOTH buttons' `.if-walk-txt` `width` to that max. Combined with the button's `justify-content:center`, the content group (glyph + equal‑width text block) is centred → the two share a left edge, the **longest is centred** and the **shorter left‑aligns** to it. Measurement‑based → portable to any names; **clears itself (text‑block width='') in the one‑row desktop layout** (detected by all buttons sharing a top) so §40's consistent‑trailing is untouched. Runs on load / `document.fonts.ready` / resize (rAF‑throttled). Verified real‑page render at 700px (`colalign2.js`): col1 (01/03) both txt=98px sharedLeft, col2 (02/04) both txt=113px sharedLeft, longest centred; desktop 1280 = txt widths cleared, trailing margins still 121px equal, 0 errors. Promoted JS `645c348`. **Dial:** nothing — fully automatic from the rendered name widths.

User: the black‑callout + divider + separate "We do that work in four stages." heading read clumsy — combine into ONE **gold full‑width section**, top text big, second text as a tight **subheader** beneath. We are the place where ideas get built." text element (`84e28788…70e9`) re‑classed (`set_style`) from `if-blacknote-txt` → new **`if-gold-head`** (Interstate 800 via the broad if‑ rule, `font-size:clamp(30px,3.8vw,50px)`, `color:#1a1a1a`, line‑height 1.08). (Avoids touching shared `if-blacknote`/`if-blacknote-txt`, still used by the stage‑03 gold band §29.)

This dropped its scroll‑red reveal (now a plain dark subheader; the child `if-mani-red` span "four stages." inherits dark). Premise callout's scroll‑red (§36‑A) unaffected.
- **Removed:** `if-stages-divider` (the 2px line) + `if-stages-arrow` (the gold ↓ — invisible on gold anyway).

- **✅ ITERATED (2026‑07‑08): section background gold → LIGHT GREY.** User: swap the gold container for the same light grey as the synthesis "loop, not a line" diagram panel. Matched exactly: `if-blacknote-sec` `background-color` → **`#faf9f8`** (native `update_style`, same hex as `if-syn-diagram`'s panel bg, confirmed via `query_styles`). Dark text (`if-gold-head`/`if-gold-sub` `#1a1a1a`) needed no change — reads fine on light grey. Class names (`if-gold-*`) are now doubly legacy (section is neither black nor gold) — left as‑is per the no‑churn‑without‑ask rule. Verified live compiled CSS: `.if-blacknote-sec{background-color:#faf9f8;…}`. Published.
- **✅ ITERATED (2026‑07‑08): faint top shadow so the section separates from the white Premise above it.** Native `update_style` on `if-blacknote-sec`: `box-shadow:0 -4px 8px -2px rgba(0,0,0,0.06)` (negative y‑offset = shadow casts UPWARD onto the preceding sibling; same 6% "faint" alpha as the header §23 and walk‑bar §41 shadows — compiles to `#0000000f`). 100% native, no shared‑CSS involvement. **`element_snapshot_tool` was down/erroring for several attempts this session (user confirmed MCP had gone down and relaunched it) — retried after relaunch and it worked for both `if-blacknote-sec` and its preceding sibling `if-mani-sec` individually, confirming neither section is visually broken, but a NEGATIVE‑offset shadow paints outside the target element's own box into the neighbour's space, and the snapshot tool crops tightly per‑element — so no single Designer snapshot can show the cross‑element bleed itself** (a whole‑`Body` snapshot to capture both at once timed out — too large). Given the property has no history of Designer‑vs‑published divergence in this project (unlike fonts/absolute‑div inflation — this is a plain self‑referencing `box-shadow`, no shared file involved), verification instead used the real published compiled CSS in the offline harness: `getComputedStyle` confirmed the exact shadow value applied, and a zoomed screenshot of the real rendered boundary (`shadow_zoom.png`, scratchpad) shows the faint band clearly at the seam. Published.

## 44. ABOUT "Walk the Factory" — current‑stage marker FINALIZED (2026‑07‑08) — native Designer + shared CSS/JS, promoted to `main`
§42 went through several reversions same‑day (current=hover‑look‑minus‑bar → removed entirely → restored). **This is the final, user‑approved model — do not re‑litigate without an explicit new ask:**
- **CURRENT** (`.is-current`, set by the `walk-spy` scrollspy module): **FULL COLOUR + a red left bar, ALWAYS — independent of hover.** `.if-walk-item.is-current{background:#fff;box-shadow:inset 3px 0 0 0 #e21833;border-left-color:#e21833;}` plus the parent‑hover‑child colour combinators now also match `.is-current` (glyph `filter:none`, num `#e21833`, name `#111`).
- **HOVER alone** (not current): **FULL COLOUR, NO bar.** Native `if-walk-item:hover` pseudo reverted to `background-color:#ffffff` ONLY (removed the `box-shadow`/`border-left-color` a prior iteration had added) — so hovering a non‑current item whitens the bg + colours the glyph/num/name (via the same combinators, now keyed off `:hover, .is-current`) but the border/shadow stay at their base grey (no bar).
- **HOVER + CURRENT**: identical to CURRENT alone (the `.is-current` rule already forces the bar; hovering a current item doesn't remove or change it) — "retains the red marker," as required.
- **Rest** (neither): base grey bg `#f2f2f2`, grey border `#e6e6e6`, greyscale glyph, muted grey num/name — unchanged.
- **`walk-spy` module restored verbatim** (idea-factory.js) — same scrollspy as §42's fixed version (threshold = `headerH + data-scroll-gap + 12`, so a just‑clicked/landed stage is always ≥ the line → the correct button reads current, not the preceding one). Fallback gap constant updated `barH+24→barH` to match the current `walk-sticky` `syncGap` semantics (flush landing, no `+24`).
- **Verified offline** (`verify_current2.js` against the fresh live compiled CSS + local shared files, `current2_state.png`): rest = grey/no‑shadow/greyscale; current‑not‑hovered = white bg + red inset shadow + red border + full colour; current+hover = identical (marker retained); hover‑alone (non‑current) = trending white/full‑colour with shadow `none` and border unchanged grey; re‑navigating to stage 04 correctly moves `.is-current` to item 4 only (`[false,false,false,true]`); 0 JS errors.
- **Promoted:** `idea-factory.css`+`idea-factory.js` → `main` (commit `7321dad`) → confirmed served by GitHub Pages (byte‑exact match to local). `stable` NOT advanced yet — advance after the user confirms live.
- **✅ FOLLOW‑UP (2026‑07‑08) — pushed the native/shared split further after the user (rightly) challenged why GitHub was involved at all.** Re‑audited `.if-walk-item.is-current`'s properties: `background-color`/`box-shadow`/`border-left-color` all target the SAME element the class is on (not a descendant) — so unlike the glyph/num/name cascade, this piece has no platform reason to live in the shared file. **Moved it to a native Designer combo class** (`create_style`, `name:"is-current"`, `parent_style_names:["if-walk-item"]`) — same pattern as the pre‑existing `if-backtotop.is-visible` (§11): JS only toggles the class name via `classList`, the look itself is a real Designer style, visible/editable in the Designer UI. Removed the now‑redundant duplicate rule from `idea-factory.css`, keeping ONLY the 3 lines that genuinely cannot be native (parent class → different child element's colour — Webflow has no selector for that). Verified: fresh compiled CSS shows `.if-walk-item.is-current{background-color:#fff;border-left-color:#e21833;box-shadow:inset 3px 0 #e21833;}` as a real Designer‑authored rule; re‑ran the full 4‑state harness against it — byte‑identical behaviour to before. Published (Designer) + promoted the trimmed CSS to `main` (commit `f968c04`) → confirmed served. **Net shared‑file footprint for this whole feature is now the minimum the platform allows: 3 lines, all provably non‑native‑representable — everything else (colours, layout, this state's own look) is native and duplicates/edits with the rest of the site.**

## 45. ABOUT stage‑header bars (01‑04) — scaled 1.5× for drama, LOCKED alignment RE‑VERIFIED at new scale (2026‑07‑08) — native Designer, PUBLISHED

User: the §31H/§31I hard‑won glyph/eyebrow/title/watermark alignment was preserved, but the text had gotten too small along the way — wanted it "bolder and more dramatic," matching an older/bigger reference screenshot (confirmed by the user to be **from later‑inserted project content, NOT the original Claude Design source docs** — used purely as a size reference, not a style reference).

- **Measured the exact scale factor from the reference image** (pixel‑measured via the transcript‑image‑extraction technique, §31's `extract_user_imgs.py`): title text height ratio = **exactly 1.5×** (78px vs 52px); eyebrow ≈1.67× and glyph ≈1.09× were noisier/confounded (the reference used a different, white‑plate glyph style, §30's diagram‑tile look, not the current on‑color transparent glyph) — went with a single clean **k=1.5** applied uniformly to every component, per the user's explicit "resize ALL components proportionally."
- **Scaled directly (native `update_style`, safe/unambiguous):** `if-stage-eyebrow` font‑size, `if-stage-title` font‑size, `if-stage-glyphimg`/`if-glyph-tall`/`if-glyph-line` width/height, `if-stage-dash` width/height, `if-stage-wm` font‑size, `if-stage-head-txt` row‑gap, `if-stage-head-row` grid‑column‑gap + grid‑template‑columns col‑1 width, `if-stage-eyebrow-row` grid‑column‑gap — all straightforward ×1.5 (Python‑computed to avoid arithmetic slips).
- **⚠️ The DEPENDENT alignment formulas (glyph `margin-top` ×3 variants, `if-stage-wm` `top`, `if-stage-head-sec` `padding-top/bottom`) do NOT scale by naively multiplying every term ×1.5** — the §31G/§31H empirical baseline‑correction constants (`+20.5px`, `18.4px`) are **local‑range curve fits**, not pure font‑metric ratios (verified: a pure `ratio×fontsize` model doesn't fit the original two calibration points at all — the fit is genuinely affine). Naive ×1.5 scaling of these flat constants left a **consistent +10 to +11px baseline error at 768/992/1440** (small but real) and, separately, a padding‑centering formula that doesn't scale cleanly either (see below). **Fix = re‑ran the EXACT §31H rigorous measurement method** (offline harness, live compiled CSS, real Interstate woff2, zero‑size baseline probe) at the NEW scale and re‑solved the flat constants from fresh data, exactly as originally done — did not try to algebra‑extrapolate the old fit outside its measured range.
  - Glyph/watermark baseline flat term: `+30.75px` (naive ×1.5) → **`+20.25px`** (refit from 768/992/1440 data) → **glyphDeltaFromBaseline now 0/0.8/‑0.5px, wmDeltaFromBaseline 0.5/0.2/‑0.1px** at 1440/992/768 — matches the ORIGINAL system's own precision (§31H reported ±0.2‑0.8px).
  - **⚠️ 430px (small/tiny breakpoint) is WORSE after the refit (‑17 to ‑33px delta)** — a uniform flat‑constant fix that zeroes 768‑1440 necessarily overshoots at 430, because at that width multiple clamps are simultaneously floored (a qualitatively different regime, not just a smaller point on the same line). **Deliberately accepted** per the user's own stated priority: *"when things start breaking responsively at lower breakpoints, it's not such a big deal but particularly for the largest browser sizes this needs to be bolder."* Also newly at risk at 430px: longer stage names ("STAGE 02 · PROVING GROUND") can wrap the eyebrow to 2 lines at the bigger font size, which the fixed‑offset formula doesn't account for — same de‑prioritized bucket.
- **Vertical centering (section padding‑top/bottom split) — separate bug, found and improved.** The RIGHT test isn't padding‑top≈padding‑bottom (the watermark's negative `top` offset makes a big top/bottom padding asymmetry *correct by design* — confirmed algebraically: increasing padding‑top shifts the watermark's gap-from-bar-top 1:1 but leaves the title's gap-from-bar-bottom untouched, so the two are independently tunable). The real invariant is **watermark's‑own visual top‑gap ≈ title's‑own visual bottom‑gap**. Naive ×1.5 scaling of `P`/`S`/`K` left these off by ~18px; re‑solved the flat `K` (`27.6px→18.8px`) from fresh measurement, cutting the imbalance roughly in half (~3‑11px residual across 768‑1440). **Left at this residual** (didn't chase full zero) — the watermark is a faint 10‑18%‑opacity background flourish, not a precision‑critical element, and the user was waiting; the primary, visually‑critical glyph‑to‑title lock is the one that's now essentially perfect.
- **Verified:** compiled CSS matches every intended value exactly (checked via live `curl` + DOTALL regex, Webflow's multi‑line rule format); full offline harness (real Interstate woff2, cropped glyph SVGs re‑generated from `project/assets/glyphs/*-oncolor.svg` with the documented §31I crop viewBoxes) at 1440/992/768/430, all 4 bars identical (no per‑bar divergence introduced); real screenshots confirm bold/dramatic look, correct glyph‑type variants (2×2, tall staircase, wide+belt) all render with their per‑type alignment intact; 0 JS errors; no horizontal overflow at any tested width. Published.
- **Side‑effect (expected, not a bug):** titles now wrap to 2 lines at MORE breakpoints than before (bigger glyph column leaves less width for bigger text within the fixed 1240px `if-stage-wrap`) — e.g. "Learn to think like / a builder." Confirmed this reads as intentional "bold and dramatic" display text, not broken layout; the glyph‑to‑baseline lock is wrapping‑proof by design (§31H) so it stays correct regardless of how many lines follow.
- **Dials:** the single scale factor is `1.5` — every clamp above was computed as `old×1.5`; to re‑scale again, recompute from these NEW values as the new "old," then RE‑RUN the empirical baseline/centering re‑measurement (do not skip that step — it's what makes this safe to repeat).

### 45a. Stage‑header glyphs — dropped the shared right‑edge alignment, all flush LEFT instead (2026‑07‑08) — native Designer, PUBLISHED. SUPERSEDES §31I's horizontal model.
User: §31I's "shared right edge" (narrow 01/02 glyphs inset to align their right edge with the wide 03/04 glyphs) is no longer wanted — "looks odd at small screen sizes" — put 01/02 flush at the LEFT margin like 03/04, and bring the text along with them, **keeping the same glyph‑to‑text spacing**.
- **Root mechanism swap:** `if-stage-head-row`'s `grid-template-columns` first value — was a FIXED width `clamp(127.5px,13.8vw,201px)` (= the widest glyph's width, forcing 01/02 to sit in a column bigger than themselves, right‑aligned via `justify-self:end` into the leftover slack) — is now **`auto`** (content‑sized: the column simply equals whatever glyph is inside it). `if-stage-glyphimg` `justify-self` reverted `end→start` (a no‑op once the column is content‑sized, but correctly documents intent).
- **Why this trivially satisfies "keep the same spacing":** CSS Grid's `grid-column-gap` (untouched, still `clamp(55.35px,5.985vw,87.225px)`) applies between column 1's right edge and column 2's left edge — once column 1 = the glyph's own width, the SAME gap value automatically lands right after EACH glyph's own right edge, no matter how narrow or wide that glyph is. Zero change needed to the gap itself.
- **Trade‑off (intentional, per user — "I no longer think [shared right edge] is so important"):** the eyebrow/title text no longer starts at the same X across all 4 bars — 01/02's (narrower) glyph means their text now starts further LEFT than 03/04's (wider) glyph's text. This deliberately drops §31I's "cross‑bar text alignment" invariant in favor of "everything hugs the left margin, consistent gap after the glyph."
- **Vertical alignment (§31H/§45's baseline lock) is completely unaffected** — confirmed algebraically (the lock only depends on glyph height + the C‑term, never column width) and empirically (glyphDeltaFromBaseline identical before/after: 0/‑0.5/‑17 at 1440/768/430, unchanged to the decimal).
- **Verified** (`verify_hfix.js`, live compiled CSS + real Interstate + cropped glyph SVGs): at every tested width (1440/768/430), all 4 bars show `glyphLeft:0` (flush left, uniform); `textGapFromGlyph` identical across all 4 bars at each width (55.3px at 430/768, 86.2px at 1440) despite glyph width varying 81↔127.5/198.7; glyph‑baseline delta unchanged; 0 JS errors, no horizontal overflow. Designer `element_snapshot` confirms both narrow (01) and wide (02) glyphs now sit flush at the bar's left margin.
- **Dial:** to bring back the shared‑right‑edge model, revert `grid-template-columns` to the fixed clamp width and `justify-self` back to `end` (values are in §31I/§45).
- **✅ FOLLOW‑UP (2026‑07‑08): glyph‑to‑text gap narrowed from "S+2G" to just "S" (one mini‑square, no extra gaps).** User: the gap (§31I's skip‑one‑square offset, originally derived as `S+2G` = one mini‑square PLUS two inter‑square gaps) reads as too far apart now — narrow it to exactly ONE mini‑square width. `if-stage-head-row` `grid-column-gap` recomputed from the same underlying glyph geometry (mini‑square `S=30` units, gap `G=8` units, out of the wide glyph's 106‑unit viewBox) but using the ratio `S/106=0.283` instead of `(S+2G)/106=0.434`: **`clamp(55.35px,5.985vw,87.225px)` → `clamp(36.08px,3.906vw,56.89px)`**. Because all 4 glyph types were originally calibrated to render their mini‑squares at the SAME physical size for a given viewport (§31/§45 sizing discipline), this ONE shared gap value correctly equals "one mini‑square" for every bar regardless of glyph width — confirmed both families independently compute to the same ~56px physical square size at 1440. Verified (`verify_gapfix.js`, live compiled CSS): `textGapFromGlyph` now 36.1px (430/768) / 56.2px (1440), uniform across all 4 bars, down from 55.3/86.2; glyph‑baseline lock completely unchanged (0/‑0.5/‑17, identical to before); 0 JS errors. Published.

### 45b. Stage‑header glyph/watermark misalignment on eyebrow WRAP — fixed with a small JS correction, not a breakpoint (2026‑07‑08) — shared JS, promoted to `main`. SUPERSEDES the tiny‑breakpoint attempt below.
User reported (with before/after screenshots) that on stage 03 specifically, at the smallest phone width, the glyph and the two text lines were misaligned — user's hypothesis was that it was still aligning to the "belt" under the glyph's mini‑squares. **Root cause was different:** the native CSS baseline‑lock formula (§45) assumes the eyebrow ("STAGE 0X · …") renders as exactly ONE line; at narrow viewports the LONGER stage names wrap to two lines, adding real height the fixed‑formula doesn't know about, so the glyph (positioned by a static `margin-top` calc) ends up too high relative to the now‑lower title. Confirmed by forcing `white-space:nowrap` in a test harness — delta snapped from −17px to −0.5px instantly, isolating the wrap (not the belt) as the cause.
- **⚠️ FIRST ATTEMPT (reverted): a Webflow "tiny" (≤479px) breakpoint override assuming exactly 2 eyebrow lines.** Worked perfectly AT 430px (the user's reported case) but a binary‑search measurement (`find_wrap_threshold.js`) showed **each stage's eyebrow actually wraps at a DIFFERENT width** — stage01 ≤450, stage02 ≤522, stage03 ≤548, stage04 ≤488 — because the four stage names are different lengths. Webflow only offers 3 fixed breakpoints (479/767/991), none of which land near 522 or 548, so a single flat breakpoint override necessarily left a gap: correct at 430, but WRONG (still assuming 1 line, now actually 2) for the longer names in the 480–548px range, and wrong the other way if pushed to the 767 breakpoint instead (stage01 doesn't wrap until 450, so it would be wrongly over‑corrected from 451–767). **No native‑breakpoint‑only solution can satisfy all four simultaneously.** Removed via `update_style` + `remove_properties` on the 4 classes' `tiny` breakpoint (verified compiled CSS shows no tiny override left — pure `main` formula at all widths).
- **REAL FIX — shared JS module `stage-header-eyebrow-fix` (idea-factory.js).** Measures the eyebrow's ACTUAL rendered height at runtime (`getBoundingClientRect().height` vs its own `font-size`, since `line-height:1`); if it's taller than one line (wrapped), nudges `.if-stage-glyphimg`'s `margin-top` and `.if-stage-wm`'s `top` down by exactly the extra height, via inline style layered on top of the native CSS value (clears any prior inline override first so it always re‑reads the CSS‑formula's *current* value, never a stale cache — correct on resize too). Runs on load, `document.fonts.ready`, and debounced `resize`. **Generalizes automatically** — no hardcoded per‑stage widths, works for 1 extra line or 2, adapts instantly if a stage name is ever edited to a different length. This is squarely a "Webflow genuinely can't do this" case (per CLAUDE.md §0/§7): aligning to a sibling's *content‑dependent* wrapped height isn't expressible as a static CSS breakpoint at all, let alone one of Webflow's 3 fixed ones.
- **Verified exhaustively** (`verify_jsfix.js`, live compiled CSS + real Interstate + cropped glyphs, 13 widths from 1440 down to 320): delta = **0 exactly** at every width where any bar has wrapped (450/430/375/320 where ALL 4 wrap; 480–522 range where only the longer‑name stages wrap while shorter ones correctly stay on the native single‑line formula) — better than the native system's own ±0.5–0.8px baseline residual. 0 JS errors at every width. (An unrelated, pre‑existing horizontal‑overflow quirk in footer elements at ≤375px was found during this check — NOT caused by this fix, out of scope, left alone.)
- **Promoted:** `idea-factory.js` → `main` (commit `c521fe6`) → confirmed served by GitHub Pages (byte‑exact). Native Designer change (tiny‑override removal) published same session. `stable` not advanced yet.
- **Lesson for future per‑stage alignment work:** when a CSS lock depends on an assumption about a sibling's line count/wrapping, do NOT reach for a breakpoint override — measure whether the wrap point is content‑length‑dependent first (it almost always is, once labels differ in length). If so, only a small runtime JS correction can be exactly right at every width; a fixed breakpoint can only be exactly right at the ONE width it was tuned for.

## 46. ABOUT synthesis diagram + "how people move through it" — proper responsive STACKING (2026‑07‑08) — native Designer, PUBLISHED
